<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Therapyside — Gestión PS 2026</title>
<style>
:root{--bg:#f5f5f0;--surface:#fff;--surface2:#f0efe9;--border:#e2e0d8;--text:#1a1a18;--text2:#6b6a65;--text3:#a09f9a;--accent:#1a5cb0;--accent-light:#dbeafe;--green:#16a34a;--red:#dc2626;--radius:8px}
*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}
body{background:var(--bg);font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;color:var(--text);min-height:100vh}
header{background:var(--surface);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);padding:0 20px;height:52px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:100;flex-wrap:wrap}
.logo{font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent);white-space:nowrap}
.logo span{color:var(--text2);font-weight:400}
#search{flex:1;max-width:300px;padding:7px 12px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;outline:none;background:var(--surface2)}
#search:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
select{padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;background:var(--surface);cursor:pointer;outline:none}
.hdr-right{margin-left:auto;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}
.upload-btn{background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;padding:7px 14px;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap}
.upload-btn:hover{opacity:.88}
.count-badge{font-size:12px;color:var(--text3);white-space:nowrap}
.tabs{background:var(--surface);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);padding:0 20px;display:flex;gap:0;overflow-x:auto}
.tab{padding:10px 16px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;color:var(--text2);cursor:pointer;border-bottom:2px solid transparent;white-space:nowrap;transition:color .15s}
.tab:hover{color:var(--text)}
.tab.active{color:var(--accent);border-bottom-color:var(--accent)}
.tab .cnt{font-size:11px;color:var(--text3);margin-left:3px}
.layout{display:flex}
.main-area{flex:1;min-width:0;padding:16px 20px}
.table-wrap{background:var(--surface);border-radius:var(--radius);border:1px solid var(--border);overflow:auto}
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;min-width:860px}
th{background:var(--surface2);padding:9px 10px;text-align:left;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;white-space:nowrap;cursor:pointer;user-select:none;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
th:hover{color:var(--text2)}
td{padding:9px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-size:13px;vertical-align:middle}
tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none}
tr:hover td{background:#fafaf7;cursor:pointer}
tr.selected td{background:var(--accent-light)}
.badge{display:inline-block;font-size:11px;font-weight:500;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:20px}
.b-on{background:#dcfce7;color:#15803d}
.b-off{background:#fee2e2;color:#dc2626}
.b-pause{background:#fef9c3;color:#854d0e}
.b-ban{background:#fee2e2;color:#7f1d1d}
.b-neutral{background:var(--surface2);color:var(--text2)}
.no-results{text-align:center;padding:50px;color:var(--text3)}
/* PANEL */
.panel{width:400px;flex-shrink:0;background:var(--surface);border-left:1px solid var(--border);height:calc(100vh - 104px);position:sticky;top:52px;overflow-y:auto;display:none;transition:all .2s}
.panel.open{display:block}
.panel-head{padding:14px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:8px}
.panel-name{font-size:15px;font-weight:600}
.panel-sub{font-size:12px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:3px}
.close-btn{background:none;border:none;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;color:var(--text3);flex-shrink:0}
.panel-body{padding:14px 16px}
.psection{margin-bottom:18px}
.psection h3{font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;margin-bottom:10px;padding-bottom:5px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.metric-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:6px;margin-bottom:6px}
.mbox{background:var(--surface2);border-radius:6px;padding:8px;text-align:center}
.mbox .mv{font-size:16px;font-weight:600}
.mbox .ml{font-size:10px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:1px}
.drow{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:4px 0;font-size:13px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.drow:last-child{border:none}
.drow .dl{color:var(--text2)}
.drow .dv{font-weight:500;text-align:right}
/* EDIT */
.edit-box{background:#f8f8f4;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:12px;margin-bottom:14px}
.edit-box h3{font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;margin-bottom:10px}
.frow{margin-bottom:9px}
.frow label{display:block;font-size:11px;color:var(--text2);margin-bottom:3px;font-weight:500}
.frow input,.frow select,.frow textarea{width:100%;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:6px 9px;font-size:13px;font-family:inherit;outline:none}
.frow input:focus,.frow select:focus,.frow textarea:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
.frow textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:60px;line-height:1.5}
.fgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px}
.save-btn{width:100%;background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;padding:8px;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer}
.save-btn:hover{opacity:.88}
.save-btn.ok{background:var(--green)!important}
/* MODAL */
.modal-bg{display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.45);z-index:300;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:20px}
.modal-bg.open{display:flex}
.modal{background:var(--surface);border-radius:12px;width:100%;max-width:540px;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.2)}
.modal-head{padding:16px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.modal-head h2{font-size:15px;font-weight:600}
.modal-head p{font-size:13px;color:var(--text2);margin-top:4px}
.modal-body{padding:18px 20px}
.uzone{border:2px dashed var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:24px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:10px;cursor:pointer;transition:border-color .2s}
.uzone:hover,.uzone.drag{border-color:var(--accent);background:var(--accent-light)}
.uzone input{display:none}
.uzone .icon{font-size:28px;margin-bottom:6px}
.uzone p{font-size:13px;color:var(--text2)}
.uzone .ok{font-size:12px;color:var(--green);margin-top:5px;font-weight:500}
.modal-foot{padding:12px 20px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end}
.btn{padding:7px 16px;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;border:none}
.btn-p{background:var(--accent);color:#fff}
.btn-s{background:var(--surface2);color:var(--text2);border:1px solid var(--border)}
.btn:hover{opacity:.85}
.prog{font-size:13px;color:var(--green);margin-top:8px;text-align:center;display:none}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<header>
  <div class="logo">Therapyside <span>/ PS 2026</span></div>
  <input id="search" type="text" placeholder="🔍 Buscar psicólogo, comentario...">
  <select id="f-status" onchange="render()">
    <option value="">Todos</option>
    <option value="active">Activos</option>
    <option value="banned">Baneados</option>
    <option value="paused">En pausa</option>
  </select>
  <select id="f-assign" onchange="render()">
    <option value="">Asignación</option>
    <option value="1">ON</option>
    <option value="0">OFF</option>
  </select>
  <div class="hdr-right">
    <span class="count-badge" id="count-badge"></span>
    <button class="upload-btn" onclick="openUpload()">⬆ Actualizar PBI</button>
  </div>
</header>

<div class="tabs">
  <div class="tab active" onclick="setTab(this,'')">Todos <span class="cnt" id="tc-all"></span></div>
  <div class="tab" onclick="setTab(this,'ESP')">🇪🇸 ESP <span class="cnt" id="tc-ESP"></span></div>
  <div class="tab" onclick="setTab(this,'UK')">🇬🇧 UK <span class="cnt" id="tc-UK"></span></div>
  <div class="tab" onclick="setTab(this,'ITA')">🇮🇹 ITA <span class="cnt" id="tc-ITA"></span></div>
  <div class="tab" onclick="setTab(this,'PT/BR')">🇵🇹 PT/BR <span class="cnt" id="tc-PTBR"></span></div>
  <div class="tab" onclick="setTab(this,'MX')">🇲🇽 MX <span class="cnt" id="tc-MX"></span></div>
</div>

<div class="layout">
<div class="main-area">
<div class="table-wrap">
<table>
  <thead><tr>
    <th onclick="sortBy('Therapist')">Psicólogo <span id="s0"></span></th>
    <th onclick="sortBy('Pais')">País <span id="s1"></span></th>
    <th onclick="sortBy('Assignment ON?')">Asign. <span id="s2"></span></th>
    <th onclick="sortBy('Delta (real)')">Delta <span id="s3"></span></th>
    <th onclick="sortBy('Puntaje Total')">Ranking <span id="s4"></span></th>
    <th onclick="sortBy('CR3 (%)')">CR3 <span id="s5"></span></th>
    <th onclick="sortBy('Banned?')">Estado <span id="s6"></span></th>
    <th onclick="sortBy('Pausa')">Pausa <span id="s7"></span></th>
    <th>Comentario</th>
  </tr></thead>
  <tbody id="tbody"></tbody>
</table>
<div class="no-results" id="no-results" style="display:none">Sin resultados.</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="panel" id="panel">
  <div class="panel-head">
    <div>
      <div class="panel-name" id="p-name"></div>
      <div class="panel-sub" id="p-sub"></div>
    </div>
    <button class="close-btn" onclick="closePanel()">✕</button>
  </div>
  <div class="panel-body">

    <div class="edit-box">
      <h3>✏️ Campos manuales</h3>
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="frow"><label>Pausa</label>
          <select id="e-pausa"><option value="0">No</option><option value="1">Sí</option></select>
        </div>
        <div class="frow"><label>Sexo</label>
          <select id="e-sexo"><option value="">—</option><option value="Hombre">Hombre</option><option value="Mujer">Mujer</option><option value="No binario">No binario</option></select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="frow"><label>Corriente</label><input id="e-corriente" placeholder="Ej: Cognitivo-conductual"></div>
      <div class="frow"><label>Especialidad</label><input id="e-especialidad" placeholder="Ej: Ansiedad, Coaching..."></div>
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="frow"><label>Idiomas</label><input id="e-idiomas" placeholder="Ej: Árabe, Inglés"></div>
        <div class="frow"><label>Coaching</label>
          <select id="e-coaching"><option value="">—</option><option value="Sí">Sí</option><option value="No">No</option></select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="frow"><label>Edad</label><input id="e-edad" type="number" placeholder="Edad"></div>
        <div class="frow"><label>Asignaciones</label><input id="e-asign" placeholder="Ej: Prioridad"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="frow"><label>Motivo de baneo</label><input id="e-ban" placeholder="Motivo..."></div>
      <div class="frow"><label>Comentario</label><textarea id="e-comment" placeholder="Notas internas..."></textarea></div>
      <button class="save-btn" id="save-btn" onclick="saveManual()">Guardar cambios</button>
    </div>

    <div class="psection">
      <h3>📊 Métricas</h3>
      <div class="metric-grid">
        <div class="mbox"><div class="mv" id="p-puntaje">—</div><div class="ml">Ranking</div></div>
        <div class="mbox"><div class="mv" id="p-cr3">—</div><div class="ml">CR3</div></div>
        <div class="mbox"><div class="mv" id="p-rapport">—</div><div class="ml">Rapport</div></div>
        <div class="mbox"><div class="mv" id="p-nps">—</div><div class="ml">NPS</div></div>
        <div class="mbox"><div class="mv" id="p-w5">—</div><div class="ml">W5</div></div>
        <div class="mbox"><div class="mv" id="p-w10">—</div><div class="ml">W10</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="psection">
      <h3>⚙️ Configuración</h3>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">Assignment ON</span><span class="dv" id="p-assign">—</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">Delta (real)</span><span class="dv" id="p-delta">—</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">TRP</span><span class="dv" id="p-trp">—</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">Availability</span><span class="dv" id="p-avail">—</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">Banned</span><span class="dv" id="p-banned">—</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">Lim. precio</span><span class="dv" id="p-lim">—</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">Trial</span><span class="dv" id="p-trial">—</span></div>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">Stripe</span><span class="dv" id="p-stripe">—</span></div>
    </div>

    <div class="psection">
      <h3>🔗 Acceso</h3>
      <div class="drow"><span class="dl">UUID</span><span class="dv" id="p-uuid" style="font-size:10px;word-break:break-all">—</span></div>
      <div style="margin-top:8px"><a id="p-url" href="#" target="_blank" style="font-size:13px;color:var(--accent)">Abrir en BackOffice ↗</a></div>
    </div>

  </div>
</div>
</div>

<!-- UPLOAD MODAL -->
<div class="modal-bg" id="upload-modal" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeUpload()">
  <div class="modal">
    <div class="modal-head">
      <h2>⬆ Actualizar datos PBI</h2>
      <p>Los campos manuales (comentarios, pausa, etc.) se conservan automáticamente.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="uzone" onclick="document.getElementById('file-ass').click()">
        <input type="file" id="file-ass" accept=".xlsx,.csv" onchange="handleFile('ass',this)">
        <div class="icon">📋</div>
        <p><strong>Ass_settings.xlsx</strong> — configuración de asignación</p>
        <div class="ok" id="ok-ass"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="uzone" onclick="document.getElementById('file-rank').click()">
        <input type="file" id="file-rank" accept=".xlsx,.csv" onchange="handleFile('rank',this)">
        <div class="icon">📊</div>
        <p><strong>Ranking.xlsx</strong> — métricas y puntuaciones</p>
        <div class="ok" id="ok-rank"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="prog" id="prog"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-foot">
      <button class="btn btn-s" onclick="closeUpload()">Cancelar</button>
      <button class="btn btn-p" id="proc-btn" disabled onclick="processUpload()">Actualizar datos</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xlsx/0.18.5/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>
<script>
var ALL = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('ps-data').textContent);
var MANUAL = ['Pausa','Sexo','Corriente','Especialidad','Comentario','Asignaciones manuales','Idiomas','Coaching','Edad','Motivo de baneo'];
var sortCol = null, sortDir = 1, activePais = '', currentPS = null;
var sortIds = ['s0','s1','s2','s3','s4','s5','s6','s7'];
var sortCols = ['Therapist','Pais','Assignment ON?','Delta (real)','Puntaje Total','CR3 (%)','Banned?','Pausa'];
var uploadedFiles = {ass:null,rank:null};

function loadSaved() {
  try {
    var s = localStorage.getItem('ps_manual_v2');
    if (!s) return;
    var m = JSON.parse(s);
    ALL.forEach(function(ps) {
      var u = ps['Ther. uuid'];
      if (m[u]) MANUAL.forEach(function(k){ if(m[u][k]!==undefined) ps[k]=m[u][k]; });
    });
  } catch(e){}
}

function saveToStorage() {
  try {
    var m = {};
    ALL.forEach(function(ps){
      var u = ps['Ther. uuid']; m[u]={};
      MANUAL.forEach(function(k){ m[u][k]=ps[k]; });
    });
    localStorage.setItem('ps_manual_v2', JSON.stringify(m));
  } catch(e){}
}

function pct(v) {
  if (v===''||v===null||v===undefined) return '—';
  var n = parseFloat(v);
  if (isNaN(n)) return v;
  return (n*100).toFixed(0)+'%';
}
function fmt(v){ return (v===''||v===null||v===undefined)?'—':v; }

function filtered() {
  var q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  var fs = document.getElementById('f-status').value;
  var fa = document.getElementById('f-assign').value;
  return ALL.filter(function(ps){
    if (activePais && ps['Pais']!==activePais) return false;
    if (fs==='active' && (ps['Banned?']==1||ps['Pausa']==1)) return false;
    if (fs==='banned' && ps['Banned?']!=1) return false;
    if (fs==='paused' && ps['Pausa']!=1) return false;
    if (fa!=='' && String(ps['Assignment ON?'])!==fa) return false;
    if (q) return (ps['Therapist']||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(ps['Comentario']||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(ps['Especialidad']||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(ps['Ther. uuid']||'').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    return true;
  });
}

function render() {
  var res = filtered();
  if (sortCol!==null) {
    var col = sortCols[sortCol];
    res.sort(function(a,b){
      var va=String(a[col]||''), vb=String(b[col]||'');
      var na=parseFloat(va), nb=parseFloat(vb);
      if(!isNaN(na)&&!isNaN(nb)) return (na-nb)*sortDir;
      return va<vb?-sortDir:va>vb?sortDir:0;
    });
  }
  // Tab counts
  document.getElementById('tc-all').textContent='('+ALL.length+')';
  ['ESP','UK','ITA','PT/BR','MX'].forEach(function(p){
    var id='tc-'+(p==='PT/BR'?'PTBR':p);
    var el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) el.textContent='('+ALL.filter(function(x){return x['Pais']===p;}).length+')';
  });
  document.getElementById('count-badge').textContent=res.length+' psicólogos';
  var tbody=document.getElementById('tbody');
  var noR=document.getElementById('no-results');
  if(!res.length){tbody.innerHTML='';noR.style.display='block';return;}
  noR.style.display='none';
  var flags={ESP:'🇪🇸',UK:'🇬🇧',ITA:'🇮🇹','PT/BR':'🇵🇹',MX:'🇲🇽'};
  tbody.innerHTML=res.map(function(ps){
    var sel=currentPS&&currentPS['Ther. uuid']===ps['Ther. uuid']?'selected':'';
    var asgn=ps['Assignment ON?']==1?'<span class="badge b-on">ON</span>':'<span class="badge b-off">OFF</span>';
    var estado=ps['Banned?']==1?'<span class="badge b-ban">Baneado</span>':'<span class="badge b-on">Activo</span>';
    var pausa=ps['Pausa']==1||ps['Pausa']==='1'?'<span class="badge b-pause">Pausa</span>':'';
    var cr3=ps['CR3 (%)']!==''?(parseFloat(ps['CR3 (%)'])*100).toFixed(0)+'%':'—';
    var ptj=ps['Puntaje Total']!==''?(parseFloat(ps['Puntaje Total'])*100).toFixed(0)+'%':'—';
    var com=ps['Comentario']?'<span style="color:var(--text2);font-size:12px">'+String(ps['Comentario']).substring(0,45)+(String(ps['Comentario']).length>45?'…':'')+'</span>':'';
    return '<tr class="'+sel+'" onclick="openPanel(''+ps['Ther. uuid']+'')">'+
      '<td style="font-weight:500">'+fmt(ps['Therapist'])+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+(flags[ps['Pais']]||'')+' '+fmt(ps['Pais'])+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+asgn+'</td>'+
      '<td style="text-align:center">'+fmt(ps['Delta (real)'])+'</td>'+
      '<td style="text-align:center">'+ptj+'</td>'+
      '<td style="text-align:center">'+cr3+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+estado+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+pausa+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+com+'</td>'+
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');
}

function sortBy(idx) {
  if(sortCol===idx) sortDir*=-1; else{sortCol=idx;sortDir=1;}
  sortIds.forEach(function(id){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent='';});
  var el=document.getElementById(sortIds[idx]);
  if(el) el.textContent=sortDir===1?' ↑':' ↓';
  render();
}

function setTab(el,pais){
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});
  el.classList.add('active'); activePais=pais; render();
}

document.getElementById('search').addEventListener('input',render);

function openPanel(uuid) {
  var ps=ALL.find(function(x){return x['Ther. uuid']===uuid;});
  if(!ps) return;
  currentPS=ps;
  document.getElementById('p-name').textContent=ps['Therapist'];
  document.getElementById('p-sub').textContent=(ps['Pais']||'')+(ps['Availability']?' · '+ps['Availability']:'');
  document.getElementById('p-puntaje').textContent=pct(ps['Puntaje Total']);
  document.getElementById('p-cr3').textContent=pct(ps['CR3 (%)']);
  document.getElementById('p-rapport').textContent=pct(ps['Rapport']);
  document.getElementById('p-nps').textContent=pct(ps['NPS Ther.']);
  document.getElementById('p-w5').textContent=pct(ps['W5']);
  document.getElementById('p-w10').textContent=pct(ps['W10']);
  document.getElementById('p-assign').textContent=ps['Assignment ON?']==1?'✅ ON':'❌ OFF';
  document.getElementById('p-delta').textContent=fmt(ps['Delta (real)']);
  document.getElementById('p-trp').textContent=fmt(ps['TRP']);
  document.getElementById('p-avail').textContent=fmt(ps['Availability']);
  document.getElementById('p-banned').textContent=ps['Banned?']==1?'⛔ Sí':'No';
  document.getElementById('p-lim').textContent=fmt(ps['Lim. precio']);
  document.getElementById('p-trial').textContent=ps['Trial?']==1?'✅ Sí':'No';
  document.getElementById('p-stripe').textContent=ps['Stripe?']==1?'✅ Sí':ps['Stripe?']==-1?'❌ No':'—';
  document.getElementById('p-uuid').textContent=ps['Ther. uuid'];
  document.getElementById('p-url').href=ps['URL']||'#';
  document.getElementById('e-pausa').value=ps['Pausa']==1||ps['Pausa']==='1'?'1':'0';
  document.getElementById('e-sexo').value=ps['Sexo']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-corriente').value=ps['Corriente']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-especialidad').value=ps['Especialidad']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-idiomas').value=ps['Idiomas']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-coaching').value=ps['Coaching']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-edad').value=ps['Edad']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-asign').value=ps['Asignaciones manuales']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-ban').value=ps['Motivo de baneo']||'';
  document.getElementById('e-comment').value=ps['Comentario']||'';
  var btn=document.getElementById('save-btn');
  btn.textContent='Guardar cambios'; btn.classList.remove('ok');
  document.getElementById('panel').classList.add('open');
  render();
}

function closePanel(){ document.getElementById('panel').classList.remove('open'); currentPS=null; render(); }

function saveManual(){
  if(!currentPS) return;
  currentPS['Pausa']=document.getElementById('e-pausa').value;
  currentPS['Sexo']=document.getElementById('e-sexo').value;
  currentPS['Corriente']=document.getElementById('e-corriente').value;
  currentPS['Especialidad']=document.getElementById('e-especialidad').value;
  currentPS['Idiomas']=document.getElementById('e-idiomas').value;
  currentPS['Coaching']=document.getElementById('e-coaching').value;
  currentPS['Edad']=document.getElementById('e-edad').value;
  currentPS['Asignaciones manuales']=document.getElementById('e-asign').value;
  currentPS['Motivo de baneo']=document.getElementById('e-ban').value;
  currentPS['Comentario']=document.getElementById('e-comment').value;
  saveToStorage();
  var btn=document.getElementById('save-btn');
  btn.textContent='✓ Guardado'; btn.classList.add('ok');
  setTimeout(function(){btn.textContent='Guardar cambios';btn.classList.remove('ok');},2000);
  render();
}

function openUpload(){ document.getElementById('upload-modal').classList.add('open'); }
function closeUpload(){
  document.getElementById('upload-modal').classList.remove('open');
  uploadedFiles={ass:null,rank:null};
  document.getElementById('ok-ass').textContent='';
  document.getElementById('ok-rank').textContent='';
  document.getElementById('proc-btn').disabled=true;
  document.getElementById('prog').style.display='none';
}

function handleFile(type,input){
  var file=input.files[0]; if(!file) return;
  uploadedFiles[type]=file;
  document.getElementById('ok-'+type).textContent='✓ '+file.name;
  if(uploadedFiles.ass&&uploadedFiles.rank) document.getElementById('proc-btn').disabled=false;
}

function processUpload(){
  var prog=document.getElementById('prog');
  prog.style.display='block'; prog.textContent='Procesando Ass_settings...';
  readXLSX(uploadedFiles.ass,function(assData){
    prog.textContent='Procesando Ranking...';
    readXLSX(uploadedFiles.rank,function(rankData){
      prog.textContent='Cruzando datos y conservando campos manuales...';
      var cmap={es:'ESP',mx:'MX',gb:'UK',en:'UK',pt:'PT/BR',br:'PT/BR',it:'ITA'};
      var rankMap={};
      rankData.forEach(function(r){ if(r['Ther. uuid']) rankMap[r['Ther. uuid']]=r; });
      var manualSaved={};
      ALL.forEach(function(ps){
        var u=ps['Ther. uuid']; manualSaved[u]={};
        MANUAL.forEach(function(k){ manualSaved[u][k]=ps[k]; });
      });
      ALL=assData.filter(function(r){return r['Ther. uuid'];}).map(function(r){
        var uuid=r['Ther. uuid'];
        var rk=rankMap[uuid]||{};
        var ps=Object.assign({},r);
        ps['CR3 (%)']=rk['CR3 (%)']!==undefined?rk['CR3 (%)']:'';
        ps['Rapport']=rk['Rapport']!==undefined?rk['Rapport']:'';
        ps['W5']=rk['W5']!==undefined?rk['W5']:'';
        ps['W10']=rk['W10']!==undefined?rk['W10']:'';
        ps['NPS Ther.']=rk['NPS Ther.']!==undefined?rk['NPS Ther.']:'';
        ps['Puntaje Total']=rk['Puntaje Total']!==undefined?rk['Puntaje Total']:'';
        ps['Lim. precio']=rk['Lim. precio']!==undefined?rk['Lim. precio']:'';
        ps['Pais']=cmap[String(ps['L']||'').toLowerCase()]||String(ps['L']||'').toUpperCase();
        var m=manualSaved[uuid]||{};
        MANUAL.forEach(function(k){ ps[k]=m[k]!==undefined?m[k]:''; });
        return ps;
      });
      saveToStorage();
      prog.textContent='✅ '+ALL.length+' psicólogos actualizados correctamente.';
      setTimeout(function(){closeUpload();render();},1500);
    });
  });
}

function readXLSX(file,cb){
  var reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=function(e){
    var wb=XLSX.read(e.target.result,{type:'array'});
    var ws=wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
    cb(XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{defval:''}));
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
}

document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape'){closePanel();closeUpload();}});
loadSaved();
render();
</script>
</body>
</html>
